Function `lp_direction(p, M, max_b=None)`:

Let's detail functions.

FFT convolution:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    need=2*n-1
    size=1<<(need-1).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size,need)  # numpy irfft n parameter? np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=size)[:need]
    return np.maximum(b,0)
```
Use `np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)[:need]`.

Smooth max and weights:
```
def smooth_max_weights(b,beta):
  m=b.max()
  if beta<=0 or m<=0: w=ones/len; return m,w
  x=beta*(b-m)
  x=np.clip(x,-700,0)
  e=np.exp(x); Z=e.sum()
  if Z<=0/w...: e=zeros; e[argmax]=1; Z=1
  w=e/Z
  G=m + log(Z)/beta
  return G,w
```

Gradient corr:
```
def conv_grad(p,w): # returns s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}
   n=len(p); m=len(w)
   need=n+m-1; size pow2
   P=rfft(p,size); WR=rfft(w[::-1],size); C=irfft(P*WR,size)[:need]
   s=C[m-n:m][::-1].copy()
   return s
```
Check indices: w length 2n-1. For i=0: s0=Σ_j p_j w_j. C[m-1? Let's test small? Previous code: s=C[m - n : m][::-1]. Good.
Then g=2*s.

Because the objective is scale invariant and p positive, multiplicative is natural. Let's define:
```
def multiplicative_refine(p, deadline, beta=20000, base_eta=10, min_eta=0.1, max_eta=100, it_limit=100000):
  p=normalize(p)
  best_p=p.copy(); best=fast_score(p)
  eta=base_eta
  no_improve=0
  while time<deadline:
    c=conv(p); G,w=smooth_max_weights(c,beta); g=2*corr(p,w)
    # optional centering to reduce scale: g -= dot(p,g)
    g = g - np.dot(p,g)
    # clip g to avoid overflow
    scale = max(abs(g).max(), 1e-12)
    h = g/scale
    # try eta values: eta, eta*0.5, eta*2? choose first improving smooth? For efficiency, candidate q=normalize(p*exp(-eta*h))
    # Since h scale 1, eta should be maybe 0.01-0.5? Original g finite? If use h, eta effectively eta*maxg. Need scale. Let's use raw g and clip.
```

Projective gradient with adaptive beta and active weights. Let's write:
```
def score_grad(p, beta):
 c=fft_convolution(p)
 if beta==0: M=c.max(); w uniform over c>=M*(1-1e-7)-1e-12; G=M
 else G,w=smooth_max_weights(c,beta)
 s=conv_corr(p,w); g=2*s
 best index? 
 return G,w,g,M
```
Projected gradient:
```
def projected_refine(p, deadline, start_beta=500, end_beta=50000, it_per_phase... ):
 p=normalize(floor)
 best_p, best_score...
 eta=1.0
 phase...
 while time<deadline:
   c=fftp; G,w=smooth; M=max
   if M* n < best_score... track
   if use active when beta large: w active
   g=2corr; d=g - np.mean(g)  # Euclidean tangent
   # clip d? 
   gmax=np.max(np.abs(d)); if gmax<1e-14: break
   # step in terms of d/gmax so step is relative max change
   for step in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
      q=project_simplex(p - step*d/gmax, 1.0)
      q=normalize(q)
      qc=fft_convolution(q); qM=qc.max(); # maybe smooth G_q
      if qM < curM*(1-1e-12) or smooth_q < G: accept
   if accept: eta=min(4,eta*1.2) else eta*=0.7
```
Step scaling: d/gmax has max 1. If step=1, subtract up to 1 from p ~1e-4 -> could zero many. Project. Maybe step=0.05 better. Use step in (0.1,0.05,...). Let's set eta base 0.2. Since p values small, relative change = step/(p_i) huge if p_i small; but project. Multiplicative may be better for positivity.

Multiplicative:
```
def multiplicative_refine(p, deadline, beta=5000):
 p=normalize(p+floor)
 best...
 eta=0.1
 while:
  c=fft; G,w=smooth; g=2corr; g-=dot(p,g)
  gscale=max(abs(g).max(),1e-15)
  h=g/gscale
  # gradient could be all positive? centered by weighted mean.
  for step in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
     logq=np.log(p) - step*h
     # avoid overflow: center
     z=logq - max(logq)
     q=exp(z); s=sum(q); if s>0 q/=s
     qc=fft(q); qG,_=smooth(qc,beta); qM=max
     if qG < G - 1e-12 and qM < best? accept
  if accept eta*=1.3 else eta*=0.7
```
Multiplicative with h max 1, step 0.1 gives relative 10% change max, okay. It stays positive. It may be slower but stable. Could accept if smooth G decreases. Use true best.

We can implement a `local_refine` that alternates multiplier and projection, with no_improve break.

Pseudo:
```
def local_refine(p, deadline, mode='both', max_iter=200000):
 p=normalize_simplex(p); p=np.maximum(p,1e-12)
 best_p=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*max(conv(p))
 mode=0
 it=0
 no_improve=0
 eta=0.1
 while time<deadline:
   # maybe every 500 switch mode
   c=fft_convolution(p)
   M=max(c); cur_score=2nM
   if cur_score<best_score: best...
   W = active_weights(c, n_actives=min(max(1, int(0.02*n)), n*2?)) # maybe
   g=2*corr(p,W)
   if mode%2==0:
      # multiplicative
      g2=g - np.dot(p,g)
      gscale=max(abs(g2).max(),1e-16); h=g2/gscale
      accepted=False
      for step in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1, eta*0.05):
         z=np.log(p) - step*h
         z-=z.max()
         q=np.exp(z)
         s=q.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         q/=s
         qc=fft(q); qM=max(qc)
         if qM < M - 1e-14 or (abs(qM-M)<1e-14 and qM < best_score...):
             p=q; M=qM; cur_score=2nM; accepted=True; break
      if accepted: eta=min(1.0, eta*1.2)
      else: eta*=0.7; if eta<1e-5: ...
   else:
      d=g - np.mean(g)
      gscale=norm? maxabs
      for step in (eta, eta*0.5,...):
         q=project(p - step*(d/gscale),1)
         ...
   if no_improve > 5000: break
   it+=1
```
Active weights: Instead of top rel_tol, maybe use smooth max for weighting to avoid confusion. But active exact may produce large step. Use beta moderate for g but true score for accept. Could combine: weights from smoothmax beta = 10*n maybe? Let's define beta weights = 10*n? c values ~1/n, difference maybe 1/n^2; β=1e4 maybe. Previous full beta 1e6. Direct local refine function:
Let's make it robust and parameterized:
```
def direct_refine(p, subdeadline, width=1e-4, step=0.05, max_iter=200000):
    p=normalize_simplex(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
    n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    eta=step
    it=0; no_improve=0
    use_multiplicative=1
    while time.monotonic()<subdeadline and it<max_iter:
       it+=1
       c=fft_convolution(p)
       M=float(c.max())
       cur_score=2.0*n*M
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 3000: break
       w=active_weights(c,width)
       g=2.0*conv_corr(p,w)
       # If using multiplicative each 2nd iteration? We'll use multiplicative primarily, maybe projection if eta fails many.
       if use_multiplicative or no_improve%1000<500:
          g = g - float(np.dot(p,g))
          gs=max(abs(g).max(),1e-16)
          h=g/gs
          accepted=False
          for mult in (1,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
             a=eta*mult
             if a<1e-7: continue
             z=np.log(p) - a*h
             z -= z.max()
             q=np.exp(z)
             s=q.sum()
             if s<=0: continue
             q/=s
             qc=fft_convolution(q)
             qM=float(qc.max())
             if qM < M - 1e-14:
                 p=q; M=qM; cur_score=2n*qM; accepted=True; break
          if accepted: eta=min(1.0, eta*1.15)
          else: eta *=0.7; if eta<1e-5: use_projection? maybe break
       else:
          d=g - float(np.mean(g))
          gs=max(np.abs(d).max(),1e-16)
          for mult...
             q=project_simplex(p - eta*mult*(d/gs), 1.0)
             q=normalize? project target 1
       if it%200==0: print
    return best_p,best_score
```

`analytic_starts` code details:
```
def analytic_starts(n,rng,prev_arr):
    starts=[]
    i=np.arange(n)
    def safe_add(p):
       if p is None: return
       q=normalize_simplex(p)
       if q.size==n and np.all(np.isfinite(q)): starts.append(q.copy())
    safe_add(np.ones(n))
    # helper power
    for a in (0.2,0.3,0.4,0.45,0.49,0.5,0.52,0.55,0.6,0.8):
       p=np.power(i+1.0, -a); add
       p=np.power(n-i, -a); add  # n-i for last i=n-1 ->1; first n
    # capped
    for c in (0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0): p=1.0/(np.sqrt(i+ c)+1.0); add; p=1.0/(np.sqrt(n-i+ c)+1.0)
    # beta fx
    x=(i+0.5)/n
    for a,b in [(-0.25,0.0),(-0.15,0.1),(0.0,0.1),(0.0,0.3),(0.2,0.0),(-0.25,0.25),(0.0,0.5),(0.3,-0.1)]:
       p=(x+1e-6)**a * ((1.0-x)+1e-6)**b; add if finite
    # linear
    for c in (0.3,0.6,0.8):
       p=1.0 + c*x; add
       p=1.0 + c*(1.0-x); add
    # base
    base=np.power(i+1.0, -0.5)
    t=x
    # deterministic cosine modulations of base
    for eps in (0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8):
      for f in (1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34):
         p=base*(1.0+eps*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t)); add if p.min()>0
         p=base*(1.0+eps*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t)); if min>0
      # chirp
      p=base*(1.0+eps*np.cos(2*np.pi*2.0*t*t)); if min>0 add
      p=base*(1.0+eps*np.sin(2*np.pi*3.0*t*t)); if min>0 add
      # high freq
      p=base*(1.0+eps*((i%2)==0).astype(float)*2-1?) Need nonnegative.
```

for eps in (0.5,0.7): p=base*(1+eps*alt); add if min>0

`local_refine` details:
```
def local_refine(p0, deadline, beta, step, mode="multi", max_iter=200000):
    p=normalize_simplex(p0)
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    p=normalize_simplex(p)
    n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy()
    c0=fft_convolution(p)
    M0=float(np.max(c0))
    best_score=2.0*n*M0
    eta=step
    it=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iter:
        it +=1
        c=fft_convolution(p)
        M=float(np.max(c))
        cur_score=2.0*n*M
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=cur_score; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
        if no_improve > 2000:
            break
        if it % 1000 ==0: print
        G,w=smooth_max_weights(c,beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G): break
        g=2.0*conv_corr(p,w)
        g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        accepted=False
        if mode == "multi":
            g2=g - float(np.dot(p,g))
            gs=float(np.max(np.abs(g2)))
            if not finite or gs<1e-16: break
            h=g2/gs
            for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                a=eta*mult
                if a <=1e-7: continue
                z=np.log(p) - a*h
                z -= float(np.max(z))
                q=np.exp(z)
                s=float(np.sum(q))
                if s<=0 or not finite: continue
                q *= 1.0/s
                qc=fft_convolution(q)
                Gq,_=smooth_max_weights(qc,beta)
                Mq=float(np.max(qc))
                if (Gq < G - 1e-12) or (abs(Gq-G)<1e-12 and Mq < M - 1e-12):
                    p=q; G=Gq; M=Mq; accepted=True; break
        else:
            d=g - float(np.mean(g))
            gs=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if gs<1e-16: break
            h=d/gs
            for mult...:
                a=eta*mult
                q=project_simplex(p - a*h, 1.0)

    return best_p,best_score

Double-check `block_objective`:
```
def block_objective(c,n,B,beta):
    c=np.asarray(c,float).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != B-1: ...
    L=n//B
    z=np.empty(B)
    z[:-1]=c; z[-1]=0
    if not finite: return inf, zeros
    z -= z.max()
    wb=np.exp(z); s=wb.sum()
    if s<=0: wb uniform
    H=wb/L
    p=np.repeat(H,L)
    b=fft_convolution(p)
    G,w=smooth_max_weights(b,beta)
    g=2*conv_corr(p,w)
    Gsum=g.reshape(B,L).sum(axis=1)
    Wavg=float(np.dot(wb,Gsum))
    gradz=(wb/L)*(Gsum-Wavg)
    grad=n*gradz[:-1]
    val=n*G
    if not finite val: val=inf, grad zeros
    grad=np.where(finite,grad,0)
    return val, grad
```
If B=1: c size0, z[-1]=0, wb[0]=1, p ones/n? Actually L=n, H=1/n, gradient empty. Gradient of constant objective zero; minimize returns. We don't use B=1.

```
def lp_rows_cols(m):
 j=np.repeat(np.arange(m),m)
 i=np.tile(np.arange(m),m)
 return j+i, j
```

`lp_refine` loops `for m in (256,512)` if budget. To avoid too long, time check. It calls `lp_direction`, which can be slow. Maybe limit total LP outer iterations to 20. Use `while time<dl and iter_count<30`.

```
def lp_refine(p_full, deadline, ms):
 best_sc=fast_score(p_full); best=p_full
 for m in ms:
   if time+30>deadline: break
   p_small=normalize(downsample)
   rows,cols=lp_rows_cols(m)
   it=0
   while time+60<deadline and it<12:
      it+=1
      c=fft(p_small); M=max(c)
      b=lp_direction(...)
      if b is None: break
      # blends
      for t in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.35,0.2):
         cand_small=normalize((1-t)*p_small + t*b)
         cand_full=upsample(cand_small,p_full.size)
         sc=fast_score(cand_full)
         if sc<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best=cand_full.copy(); p_small=cand_small.copy()
      # after trying, if no improvement maybe break? Continue with same p_small? If no improve, direction maybe not helpful; break outer?
      if not improved: break
 return best
```
Need `improved` flag reset each iteration.

Upsampling via `resize_array` calls linspace each time; expensive. Precompute x_old? But top-level simple. LP maybe 20 upsample * 8192, okay. `np.interp` 8192 length 20 times fine.

`downsample` when n divisible: `p_full.reshape(n//m, m).mean(axis=0)`. For N=8192,m=512, reshape(16,512). Sum preserved; if p length not multiple, resize.

Now, we should think about `lp_direction` objective maximizing sum b; if using upper bound 50, a feasible direction might be blocked. For p sum1, b sum can be at most? Constraints C b ≤ M. Since C has positive entries. If p min ~1/n ~1e-4, M~1/n~1e-4. Row 0 constraint p0 b0≤M; if p0~1e-4, b0≤1. Other rows with multiple p entries bound sum by M/(min p * overlap), maybe ≤ n? For uniform p M=1/n, row center has n entries of 1/n, constraint b_sum? The center row sum of b over all j = sum b ≤ M / (1/n) =1. ```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(a * a) / (sum(a))^2

where a*a is linear convolution.  The objective is scale invariant, so the
search normalizes to sum(a)=1 and minimizes 2n*max(a*a).

Strategy:
  - Generate many structured starts around the strong one-sided
    half-power shape p_i ~ (i+1)^(-1/2), including smooth random
    multiplicative modulations, chirps, and (if provided) the previous
    best construction.
  - Optimize low-dimensional piecewise-constant log-weight models with
    L-BFGS-B to reshape promising starts.
  - Refine full lengths by projected/multiplicative subgradient-style
    descent using FFT-convolution and smooth-max weights.
  - Periodically run a downsampled LP direction inspired by the previous
    state-of-the-art: maximize sum(b) subject to p*b <= current peak.
  - Keep the best sequence found and verify it with evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LP = False


def fft_convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a via FFT, clipped to remove tiny negatives."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([a[0] * a[0]], dtype=np.float64)

    need = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)[:need]
    b = np.ascontiguousarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def normalize_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    """Clip to nonnegative and scale to a fixed sum."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(v))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    if target > 0.0:
        v *= target / s
    else:
        v[:] = 0.0
    return v


def smooth_max_weights(b, beta):
    """Smooth maximum of b and normalized softmax weights."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    mlen = b.size
    if mlen == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(mlen, dtype=np.float64)
        w /= float(mlen)
        return m, w

    x = beta * (b - m)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    Z = float(np.sum(e))

    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        e = np.zeros(mlen, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        Z = 1.0

    w = e / Z
    G = m + float(np.log(Z)) / beta
    return float(G), w


def conv_corr(p, w):
    """
    Return s_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, needed for derivatives of
    convolution coefficients with respect to p.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(n, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(p[0] * w[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    need = n + m - 1
    size = 1 << (need - 1).bit_length()

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    C = np.fft.irfft(P * WR, size)[:need]

    s = C[m - n:m][::-1].copy()
    return np.ascontiguousarray(s, dtype=np.float64)


def fast_score(p):
    """Scale-invariant fast objective using FFT."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return float("inf")

    q = p / S
    c = fft_convolution(q)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    """Project onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)

    low = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    high = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0

    for _ in range(50):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        s = float(np.sum(np.maximum(v - mid, 0.0)))
        if s > target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (low + high)
    q = np.maximum(v - tau, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(q))

    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        q = np.full(v.shape, target / float(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q *= target / s

    return q


def block_to_p(n, B, c):
    """Convert relative block log-weights to a full length-n sequence."""
    L = n // B
    z = np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if B > 1:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        tmp = np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        mm = min(tmp.size, c.size)
        tmp[:mm] = c[:mm]
        c = tmp
        z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.zeros(B, dtype=np.float64)

    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    wb = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(wb))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb /= s

    H = wb / float(L)
    return np.repeat(H, L)


def p_to_block(p, n, B):
    """Extract relative block log-weights by averaging current p per block."""
    L = n // B
    if L <= 0 or B <= 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(L, B)
    H = np.mean(p, axis=0)
    H = np.maximum(H, 1e-18)
    logH = np.log(H)
    c = logH[:-1] - logH[-1]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.clip(c, -10.0, 10.0).astype(np.float64)


def block_objective(c, n, B, beta):
    """Objective and gradient for block-constant log-weights."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != max(0, B - 1):
        tmp = np.zeros(max(0, B - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        mm = min(tmp.size, c.size)
        tmp[:mm] = c[:mm]
        c = tmp

    L = n // B
    z = np.empty(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if B > 1:
        z[:-1] = c
    z[-1] = 0.0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    wb = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(wb))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        wb = np.ones(B, dtype=np.float64) / float(B)
    else:
        wb /= s

    H = wb / float(L)
    p = np.repeat(H, L)

    b = fft_convolution(p)
    G, w = smooth_max_weights(b, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(G):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(B - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    svec = conv_corr(p, w)
    g = 2.0 * svec
    Gsum = g.reshape(B, L).sum(axis=1)
    Wavg = float(np.dot(wb, Gsum))
    gradz = (wb / float(L)) * (Gsum - Wavg)

    grad = n * gradz[:-1]
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    val = float(n * G)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = float("inf")
    return val, grad.astype(np.float64)


def run_block_minimize(c0, n, B, beta, bounds, maxiter=60):
    """Robust L-BFGS-B on block log-weights."""
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64)

    c0 = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c0.size != max(0, B - 1):
        tmp = np.zeros(max(0, B - 1), dtype=np.float64)
        mm = min(tmp.size, c0.size)
        tmp[:mm] = c0[:mm]
        c0 = tmp

    c0 = np.clip(c0, -10.0, 10.0)

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-5,
        "maxls": 30,
        "maxcor": 16,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            block_objective,
            c0,
            args=(n, B, beta),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
        )
        c = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        c = c0

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = c0

    c = np.clip(c, -10.0, 10.0)
    return c.astype(np.float64)


def random_block_init(B, rng, scale=2.0):
    """Random smooth-ish block log-weight start."""
    if B <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    c = rng.normal(0.0, scale, B - 1).astype(np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, B - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = 0.5 * c + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 2.0 * t)
    return np.clip(c, -8.0, 8.0).astype(np.float64)


def optimize_blocks(p0, n, deadline, rng):
    """Successively optimize piecewise-constant models from one start."""
    best_p = normalize_simplex(p0)
    best_sc = fast_score(best_p)
    print(f"block start score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    for B in (16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512):
        if n % B != 0 or B >= n:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
            break

        maxiter = 70 if B <= 128 else 55
        bounds = [(-8.0, 8.0)] * (B - 1)

        c0 = p_to_block(best_p, n, B)
        c = run_block_minimize(c0, n, B, 200000.0, bounds, maxiter=maxiter)
        p2 = block_to_p(n, B, c)
        sc2 = fast_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            print(f"block B={B} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

        if B <= 128 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            for _ in range(2):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                cr = random_block_init(B, rng, 2.5)
                c = run_block_minimize(cr, n, B, 80000.0, bounds, maxiter=35)
                p2 = block_to_p(n, B, c)
                sc2 = fast_score(p2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    print(f"block random B={B} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    return best_p, best_sc


def local_refine(p0, deadline, beta, step, mode, rng, max_iter=500000):
    """
    Full-length local refinement.

    mode = "multi"  : multiplicative descent on the simplex.
    mode = "proj"   : Euclidean projected subgradient descent.
    """
    p = normalize_simplex(p0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
    p = normalize_simplex(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")

    best_p = p.copy()
    c0 = fft_convolution(p)
    M0 = float(np.max(c0))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M0)

    eta = float(step)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iter:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break

        c = fft_convolution(p)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 2500:
            break

        if no_improve == 1200 and rng is not None:
            kick = np.exp(0.06 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
            p = normalize_simplex(p * kick)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            print(
                f"local mode={mode} beta={beta} it={it} best={best_score:.8f}",
                flush=True,
            )

        G, w = smooth_max_weights(c, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(G):
            break

        g = 2.0 * conv_corr(p, w)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

        accepted = False

        if mode == "multi":
            g2 = g - float(np.dot(p, g))
            gs = float(np.max(np.abs(g2)))
            if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs < 1e-16:
                break
            h = g2 / gs

            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                a = eta * mult
                if a <= 1e-7:
                    continue
                z = np.log(p) - a * h
                z -= float(np.max(z))
                q = np.exp(z)
                s = float(np.sum(q))
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                q *= 1.0 / s

                qc = fft_convolution(q)
                Gq, _ = smooth_max_weights(qc, beta)
                Mq = float(np.max(qc))

                if (Gq < G - 1e-13) or (abs(Gq - G) <= 1e-13 and Mq < M - 1e-13):
                    p = q
                    G = Gq
                    M = Mq
                    accepted = True
                    break

        else:
            d = g - float(np.mean(g))
            gs = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs < 1e-16:
                break
            h = d / gs

            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                a = eta * mult
                if a <= 1e-7:
                    continue
                q = project_simplex(p - a * h, 1.0)

                qc = fft_convolution(q)
                Gq, _ = smooth_max_weights(qc, beta)
                Mq = float(np.max(qc))

                if (Gq < G - 1e-13) or (abs(Gq - G) <= 1e-13 and Mq < M - 1e-13):
                    p = q
                    G = Gq
                    M = Mq
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if accepted:
            eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.15)
        else:
            eta *= 0.72
            if eta < 1e-5:
                eta = max(float(step), 0.1)

    return best_p, best_score


def resize_array(a, n):
    """Linearly resize a one-dimensional array to length n and normalize."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return normalize_simplex(a)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]) if np.isfinite(a[0]) else 0.0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_simplex(y)


def lp_rows_cols(m):
    """Rows/columns for the convolution matrix C with columns as b_j."""
    j = np.repeat(np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64), m)
    i = np.tile(np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64), m)
    rows = j + i
    cols = j
    return rows, cols


def lp_direction(p_small, M, upper=50.0):
    """
    Solve max sum(b) s.t. b >= 0, 0 <= b <= upper, p_small*b <= M.
    """
    if not HAVE_LP:
        return None

    p_small = np.asarray(p_small, dtype=np.float64)
    m = p_small.size
    if m <= 1 or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    rows, cols = lp_rows_cols(m)
    data = np.repeat(p_small, m)
    data = np.where(np.isfinite(data), data, 0.0)

    try:
        A = sp.csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * m - 1, m))
    except Exception:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * m - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, float(upper))] * m

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "maxiter": 20000},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None

    b /= s
    return b


def downsample(p_full, m):
    """Downsample to length m while preserving sum."""
    p_full = np.asarray(p_full, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p_full.size
    if n == m:
        return normalize_simplex(p_full)
    if n % m == 0:
        block = n // m
        q = p_full.reshape(block, m).mean(axis=0)
    else:
        q = resize_array(p_full, m)
    return normalize_simplex(q)


def upsample(p_small, n):
    """Upsample to length n using linear interpolation."""
    return resize_array(p_small, n)


def lp_refine(p_full, deadline, m_list=(256, 512)):
    """Downsampled LP direction search, upsampled back to full length."""
    best_p = normalize_simplex(p_full)
    best_sc = fast_score(best_p)
    n = best_p.size

    for m in m_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 90.0:
            break
        if m >= n or m < 16:
            continue

        p_small = downsample(best_p, m)
        improved_outer = False
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 60.0:
                break

            c = fft_convolution(p_small)
            M = float(np.max(c))
            b = lp_direction(p_small, M)
            if b is None:
                break

            best_this = None
            best_this_sc = None

            for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25):
                cand_small = (1.0 - t) * p_small + t * b
                cand_small = normalize_simplex(cand_small)
                cand_full = upsample(cand_small, n)
                sc = fast_score(cand_full)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and (best_this_sc is None or sc < best_this_sc):
                    best_this_sc = sc
                    best_this = cand_small.copy()

            if best_this is not None and best_this_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = best_this_sc
                p_small = best_this.copy()
                best_p = upsample(p_small, n)
                print(f"LP m={m} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
                improved_outer = True

            if not improved_outer:
                break
            improved_outer = False

        if best_sc < fast_score(downsample(up
sample(best_p, n), m)) * 0.999:
            pass

    return best_p, best_sc


def append_start(starts, p, n):
    """Normalize and append a valid start."""
    if p is None:
        return
    q = normalize_simplex(p)
    if q.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        starts.append(q.copy())


def analytic_starts(n, rng, prev_arr=None):
    """Generate a diverse pool of nonnegative starts."""
    starts = []
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (i + 0.5) / float(n)

    # Uniform fallback.
    append_start(starts, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    # Previous construction, if it exists and is valid.
    if prev_arr is not None:
        pa = resize_array(prev_arr, n)
        append_start(starts, pa, n)
        for _ in range(6):
            zc = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n // 2 + 1) + 1j * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n // 2 + 1)
            k = np.fft.rfftfreq(n) * n
            zc *= 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.18 * np.abs(k))
            zc[0] = 0.0
            z = np.fft.irfft(zc, n)
            z = (z - float(np.mean(z))) / (float(np.std(z)) + 1e-12)
            amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.85)
            append_start(starts, pa * np.exp(amp * np.tanh(z)), n)

    # One-sided powers: the main base family.
    for alpha in (0.20, 0.30, 0.40, 0.45, 0.48, 0.49, 0.50, 0.52, 0.55, 0.60, 0.70):
        p = np.power(i + 1.0, -alpha)
        append_start(starts, p, n)
        p = np.power(n - i, -alpha)
        append_start(starts, p, n)

    # Capped square-root shapes.
    for c in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0):
        p = 1.0 / (np.sqrt(i + c) + 1.0)
        append_start(starts, p, n)
        p = 1.0 / (np.sqrt(n - i + c) + 1.0)
        append_start(starts, p, n)

    # Beta-like endpoint shapes.
    beta_pairs = (
        (-0.25, 0.0),
        (-0.15, 0.1),
        (-0.10, 0.2),
        (0.0, 0.1),
        (0.0, 0.3),
        (0.2, 0.0),
        (0.3, -0.1),
        (-0.25, 0.25),
        (0.0, 0.5),
    )
    eps = 1e-6
    for a, b in beta_pairs:
        p = np.power(x + eps, a) * np.power((1.0 - x) + eps, b)
        append_start(starts, p, n)
        p = np.power((1.0 - x) + eps, a) * np.power(x + eps, b)
        append_start(starts, p, n)

    # Simple ramps.
    for c in (0.3, 0.6, 0.8):
        append_start(starts, 1.0 + c * x, n)
        append_start(starts, 1.0 + c * (1.0 - x), n)

    base = np.power(i + 1.0, -0.5)

    # Deterministic regular modulations of the half-power base.
    alt = 1.0 - 2.0 * (np.arange(n) % 2)
    for eps_mod in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8):
        for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21, 34):
            p = base * (1.0 + eps_mod * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x))
            if p.size == n and float(np.min(p)) > 0.0:
                append_start(starts, p, n)
            p = base * (1.0 + eps_mod * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x))
            if p.size == n and float(np.min(p)) > 0.0:
                append_start(starts, p, n)

        p = base * (1.0 + eps_mod * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 2.0 * x * x))
        if p.size == n and float(np.min(p)) > 0.0:
            append_start(starts, p, n)
        p = base * (1.0 + eps_mod * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * x * x))
        if p.size == n and float(np.min(p)) > 0.0:
            append_start(starts, p, n)

        p = base * (1.0 + eps_mod * alt)
        if p.size == n and float(np.min(p)) > 0.0:
            append_start(starts, p, n)

    # Smooth random multiplicative modulations of the base.
    for _ in range(32):
        zc = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n // 2 + 1) + 1j * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n // 2 + 1)
        k = np.fft.rfftfreq(n) * n
        zc *= 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.15 * np.abs(k))
        zc[0] = 0.0
        z = np.fft.irfft(zc, n)
        z = (z - float(np.mean(z))) / (float(np.std(z)) + 1e-12)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.95)
        append_start(starts, base * np.exp(amp * np.tanh(z)), n)

    # Slightly rougher positive modulations.
    for _ in range(12):
        fac = np.exp(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6, n))
        append_start(starts, base * fac, n)

    # Plain random positive starts for diversity.
    for _ in range(6):
        p = rng.uniform(0.05, 1.0, n)
        append_start(starts, p, n)

    if not starts:
        starts.append(np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64))

    return starts


def choose_top(starts, k):
    """Return the k lowest fast_score starts."""
    if not starts:
        return []

    scored = []
    for idx, p in enumerate(starts):
        sc = fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, idx))

    scored.sort()
    out = []
    for _, idx in scored[:k]:
        out.append(starts[idx])
    return out


def official_score(seq):
    """Safely call the authoritative evaluator."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search function invoked by the evaluator harness.

    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]

    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if budget_s < 120.0:
        N = 2048
        reserve = 25.0
    elif budget_s < 700.0:
        N = 4096
        reserve = 60.0
    else:
        N = 8192
        reserve = 130.0

    full_deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    prev_arr = None
    try:
        prev = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if prev is not None:
            arr = np.asarray(prev, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(arr)):
                prev_arr = arr
    except Exception:
        prev_arr = None

    print(f"starting search N={N} budget={budget_s:.1f}s", flush=True)

    starts = analytic_starts(N, rng, prev_arr)
    top = choose_top(starts, 10 if budget_s > 1200 else 6)
    if not top:
        top = [np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)]

    best_p = top[0]
    best_sc = fast_score(best_p)
    print(f"initial best score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    candidates = [best_p.copy()]

    # Phase 1: block-constant structural optimization from the best starts.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 120.0:
        block_alloc = min((full_deadline - t0) * 0.18, 360.0, max(60.0, budget_s * 0.18))
        block_dl = t0 + block_alloc
        block_dl = min(block_dl, full_deadline)

        if time.monotonic() < block_dl - 60.0:
            n_starts = min(2 if budget_s > 1500 else 1, len(top))
            per = max(60.0, (block_dl - time.monotonic()) / max(1, n_starts))
            for idx in range(n_starts):
                if time.monotonic() >= block_dl - 30.0:
                    break
                p0 = top[idx]
                p1, sc1 = optimize_blocks(p0, N, time.monotonic() + per, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc1
                    best_p = p1.copy()
                    candidates.append(best_p.copy())
                    print(f"after block refine idx={idx} score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 2: smooth, broad full-length refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 120.0:
        alloc = min((full_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.32, 850.0)
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        p1, sc1 = local_refine(best_p, dl, beta=10000.0, step=0.25, mode="multi", rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_p = p1.copy()
            candidates.append(best_p.copy())
            print(f"after broad local score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 3: sharper full-length refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 120.0:
        alloc = min((full_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.24, 600.0)
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        p1, sc1 = local_refine(best_p, dl, beta=200000.0, step=0.10, mode="multi", rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_p = p1.copy()
            candidates.append(best_p.copy())
            print(f"after sharp local score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 4: downsampled LP direction search for a different basin.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 180.0:
        alloc = min((full_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18, 380.0)
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        p1, sc1 = lp_refine(best_p, dl, m_list=(256, 512))
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_p = p1.copy()
            candidates.append(best_p.copy())
            print(f"after LP score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 5: very sharp projection refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 90.0:
        alloc = min((full_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.14, 360.0)
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        p1, sc1 = local_refine(best_p, dl, beta=1000000.0, step=0.08, mode="proj", rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_p = p1.copy()
            candidates.append(best_p.copy())
            print(f"after projection score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Phase 6: use remaining time for final sharp multiplicative polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < full_deadline - 20.0:
        p1, sc1 = local_refine(best_p, full_deadline, beta=1000000.0, step=0.05, mode="multi", rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_p = p1.copy()
            candidates.append(best_p.copy())
            print(f"after final polish score={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Final authoritative selection among a few candidates.
    final_candidates = list(candidates[-6:])
    if best_p is not None:
        final_candidates.append(best_p.copy())

    best_val = float("inf")
    best_seq = [1.0] * N

    # Always consider uniform sequence as a valid fallback.
    uniform_seq = [1.0] * N
    uv = official_score(uniform_seq)
    best_val = uv
    best_seq = uniform_seq

    for cand in final_candidates:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if arr.size != N:
                arr = resize_array(arr, N)
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
            s = float(np.sum(arr))
            if s <= 0.01:
                continue
            arr *= 1.0 / s
            seq = [float(v) for v in arr]
            val = official_score(seq)
            if val < best_val:
                best_val = val
                best_seq = seq
                print(f"official best={val:.8f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            continue

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val > 2.0 + 1e-9:
        return uniform_seq

    return best_seq
```
